### 10. Reuse Matrix — `MTX-REUSE`

every unit consumed unchanged or with extension

- **Mode** — BOUND
- **Canonical owner** — `00-MASTER/URRC-000001/03-REUSE-MATRIX.md`
- **Owner resolves** — YES
- **Rows emitted here** — 0 (reuse, not restatement)

### 11. Gap Matrix — `MTX-GAP`

every measured absence — missing owner, verification, certification, evidence, dependency or runtime

- **Mode** — BOUND
- **Canonical owner** — `00-MASTER/URRC-000001/06-GAP-MATRIX.md`
- **Owner resolves** — YES
- **Rows emitted here** — 0 (reuse, not restatement)

### 12. Risk Matrix — `MTX-RISK`

the compliance violations classified by severity

- **Mode** — DERIVED
- **Nearest existing owner** — `06-IMPLEMENTATION-RISK-ASSESSMENT.md` — located, but not in machine-readable form, so it cannot be bound by pointer
- **Columns** — `id`, `severity`, `finding`, `remediation`

| ID | Severity | Finding | Remediation |
|---|---|---|---|
| — | — | — | — |

### 13. Benefit Matrix — `MTX-BENEFIT`

what each disposition class delivers, measured by the units it covers

- **Mode** — DERIVED
- **Nearest existing owner** — none located
- **Columns** — `disposition`, `units`, `benefit`

| Disposition | Units | What it delivers |
|---|---|---|
| **REUSE** | 296 | the capability exists, is implemented and governed, and is consumed unchanged |
| **EXTEND** | 93 | the capability exists and is consumed, but the blueprint requires surface it does not yet provide |
| **CONFIGURE** | 42 | the capability exists and is complete; what is required is a declaration change, not code |
| **IMPLEMENT** | 2 | the capability is declared or specified but no executable realization is located |
| **MERGE** | 0 | two or more located units carry the same responsibility and must become one |
| **SUPERSEDE** | 0 | a newer located unit already discharges the responsibility; the older is bound to it |
| **DEPRECATE** | 0 | the unit is located but no consumer, interface or registration depends on it, and its responsibility is held elsewhere |
| **REMOVE** | 0 | the unit is located, carries no responsibility, no consumer and no evidence, and removal destroys nothing |

> Benefit is reported as the coverage each disposition achieves. A monetary or user-facing benefit figure is declared non-derivable and counted, not estimated.

### 14. Impact Matrix — `MTX-IMPACT`

the blast radius of each change disposition, measured as dependent units

- **Mode** — BOUND
- **Canonical owner** — `00-MASTER/UAKOS-CLOSURE-002/41-REPOSITORY-IMPACT-ASSESSMENT.md`
- **Owner resolves** — YES
- **Rows emitted here** — 0 (reuse, not restatement)

### 15. Priority Matrix — `MTX-PRIORITY`

the computed priority of every queued unit

- **Mode** — BOUND
- **Canonical owner** — `00-MASTER/UAKOS-CLOSURE-002/42-PRIORITY-REGISTER.md`
- **Owner resolves** — YES
- **Rows emitted here** — 0 (reuse, not restatement)

### 16. Execution Matrix — `MTX-EXECUTION`

the dependency-safe wave each queued unit occupies

- **Mode** — BOUND
- **Canonical owner** — `intelligence/UCOS-RIE-EXECUTION-FRONTIER.json`
- **Owner resolves** — YES
- **Rows emitted here** — 0 (reuse, not restatement)


---

*This blueprint is DERIVED TRUTH. It creates no authority, allocates no permanent corpus identity, ratifies nothing, freezes nothing and supersedes no governing instrument. Every matrix marked BOUND is a pointer to the owner that already holds it and carries no row of its own; every matrix marked DERIVED is computed from declared substrate. Every capability disposition is the output of exactly one ordered rule, and the rule is named beside it. Where a fact could not be honestly derived, the absence is declared and counted rather than filled in. Where this document conflicts with a higher frozen or governing instrument, the higher instrument governs.*
